# Coverage

Generated by `uv run dbspecs coverage` (also after every `dbspecs test`) - do not edit.
Test results: 38 targets, runs from 2026-11-25 to 2026-11-26.
Planned items come from [roadmap.yaml](roadmap.yaml).

✅ tested against real servers · ◐ partly: some versions missing or unsupported · 📄 documented, no automated test · ➖ not applicable: the engine has no such feature · ⬜ missing · ❓ not researched · ❌ test failed in the last run

### manticore 25.0.0: open and planned

| status | what | items | examples |
|---|---|---|---|
| open | Kafka sources and materialized views (Buddy): SHOW SOURCES / SHOW MATERIALIZED VIEWS work (observed 32.1.3); introspection.list-views has a gap for them, view-ddl no implementation. | 8 | `docs:SQL commands / Schema management: CREATE SOURCE`; `docs:SQL commands / Schema management: CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW`; `docs:SQL commands / Schema management: CREATE MV` |
| open | No transaction-control spec: transactions.transactional-ddl only mentions BEGIN / COMMIT / ROLLBACK in a note; START TRANSACTION and SET AUTOCOMMIT = 0 (observed: changes autocommit) are not described. | 7 | `sphinxql:start_transaction: BEGIN`; `sphinxql:start_transaction: START TRANSACTION`; `sphinxql:transact_op: COMMIT` |
| open | session.cancel-query: KILL <connid> marks the session killed (@@system.sessions.killed = 1) but an idle connection keeps working, so terminate is not possible; cancelling a running SELECT is untested (KILL on DEBUG SLEEP returned OK without effect). | 3 | `sphinxql_second:kill_connid: KILL <opt_word_query> <const_int>`; `sphinxql_second:opt_word_query: QUERY`; `docs-page:Node_info_and_management/KILL: KILL` |

| open | SELECT * FROM t.@table (schema as a result set) crashes 32.1.3 (signal 11, see engine notes); use DESCRIBE. | 1 | `docs-page:Listing_tables: SELECT FROM name.@table` |
| open | Backticked JSON keys with special characters (j.`a=b`) are not in the select grammar yet. | 1 | `docs-page:Searching/Full_text_matching/Escaping: Escaping json node names in SQL` |

### manticore 32.1.3: open and planned

| status | what | items | examples |
|---|---|---|---|
| open | Authentication (users, GRANT, tokens) exists since the auth feature; introspection.list-users / list-privileges need a test server with auth enabled (32.1.3 crashed on GRANT with auth disabled). | 32 | `sphinxql:create_user_stmt: CREATE USER <quoted_string> IDENTIFIED BY <quoted_string>`; `sphinxql:drop_user_stmt: DROP USER <quoted_string>`; `sphinxql:grant_stmt: GRANT <ident> ON <grant_target> TO <quoted_string> <opt_grant_options>` |
| open | Conversational search (Buddy): CHAT MODEL objects (CREATE / SHOW / DESCRIBE / DROP CHAT MODEL) and CALL CHAT - no specs yet. | 14 | `docs-page:Searching/Conversational_search: Conversational search`; `docs-page:Searching/Conversational_search: Before you start`; `docs-page:Searching/Conversational_search: How it works` |
| open | Kafka sources and materialized views (Buddy): SHOW SOURCES / SHOW MATERIALIZED VIEWS work (observed 32.1.3); introspection.list-views has a gap for them, view-ddl no implementation. | 8 | `docs:SQL commands / Schema management: CREATE SOURCE`; `docs:SQL commands / Schema management: CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW`; `docs:SQL commands / Schema management: CREATE MV` |
| open | No transaction-control spec: transactions.transactional-ddl only mentions BEGIN / COMMIT / ROLLBACK in a note; START TRANSACTION and SET AUTOCOMMIT = 0 (observed: changes autocommit) are not described. | 7 | `sphinxql:start_transaction: BEGIN`; `sphinxql:start_transaction: START TRANSACTION`; `sphinxql:transact_op: COMMIT` |
| open | session.cancel-query: KILL <connid> marks the session killed (@@system.sessions.killed = 1) but an idle connection keeps working, so terminate is not possible; cancelling a running SELECT is untested (KILL on DEBUG SLEEP returned OK without effect). | 3 | `sphinxql_second:kill_connid: KILL <opt_word_query> <const_int>`; `sphinxql_second:opt_word_query: QUERY`; `docs-page:Node_info_and_management/KILL: KILL` |
| open | User management needs a server with authentication enabled (see introspection.list-users gaps). | 2 | `sphinxql:set_stmt: SET PASSWORD <quoted_string>`; `sphinxql:set_stmt: SET PASSWORD <quoted_string> FOR <quoted_string>` |

| open | SELECT * FROM t.@table (schema as a result set) crashes 32.1.3 (signal 11, see engine notes); use DESCRIBE. | 1 | `docs-page:Listing_tables: SELECT FROM name.@table` |
| open | Backticked JSON keys with special characters (j.`a=b`) are not in the select grammar yet. | 1 | `docs-page:Searching/Full_text_matching/Escaping: Escaping json node names in SQL` |
